## Board Cards

### delete

`boardCards.delete`

Remove a card from the board.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.boardCards.delete({
	path: { id: '1' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `STATUS` | `string \| number \| boolean \| null` | No | — |

### list

`boardCards.list`

List the cards in a board column.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.boardCards.list({
	path: { id: '1' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  page?: number,
  pageSize?: number,
  showDeleted?: boolean,
  deletedAfterDate?: string,
  updatedAfterDate?: string,
  searchTerm?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `people` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `STATUS` | `string \| number \| boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `column` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `cards` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

### move

`boardCards.move`

Move a card to another column.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.boardCards.move({
	path: { id: 1 },
	body: { cardId: 1, columnId: 2, positionAfterId: 0 },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  cardId?: number,
  positionAfterId?: number,
  columnId?: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** _empty object_

### update

`boardCards.update`

Update a board card.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.boardCards.update({
	path: { id: '1' },
	body: {
		card: { due-date: '20261130', priority: 'high' },
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  card?: {
    content?: string,
    taskListId?: string,
    start-date?: string,
    due-date?: string,
    responsible-party-id?: string,
    estimated-minutes?: number,
    priority?: string,
    progress?: string,
    tags?: string
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `STATUS` | `string \| number \| boolean \| null` | No | — |

## Board Columns

### create

`boardColumns.create`

Create a board column.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.boardColumns.create({
	path: { id: 1470497 },
	body: {
		column: { name: 'In review', color: '#4461D7' },
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts

  column?: {
    name?: string,
    color?: string
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| number \| boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `STATUS` | `string \| number \| boolean \| null` | No | — |

### list

`boardColumns.list`

List the board columns of a project (needs Boards on for the project).

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.boardColumns.list({
	path: { id: 1 },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>

```ts

  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  page?: number,
  pageSize?: number,
  showDeleted?: boolean,
  sortBy?: string,
  sortOrder?: asc | desc,
  searchTerm?: string,
  getEmoji?: boolean,
  includeDefaults?: boolean,
  get?: string,
  updatedAfterDate?: string,
  getStats?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Comments

### create

`comments.create`

Comment on a task, milestone, file, notebook or link. Teamwork notifies the people in notify.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.comments.create({
	path: { resource: 'tasks', resourceId: '51453032' },
	body: {
		comment: { body: 'Looks good, ship it.', notify: '' },
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  resource: string,
  resourceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  comment?: {
    body?: string,
    notify?: string,
    isprivate?: boolean,
    pendingFileAttachments?: string,
    content-type?: string
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| number \| boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `STATUS` | `string \| number \| boolean \| null` | No | — |



---

### create

`people.create`

Add a user to the site. Teamwork emails them an invitation unless sendInvite is false.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.people.create({
	body: {
		person: {
			email-address: 'sam@acme.example',
			first-name: 'Sam',
			last-name: 'Lee',
			sendInvite: false,
		},
	},
});
```

### listStarred

`projects.listStarred`

List the projects the current user has starred.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts

```

## Project Updates

### create

`projectUpdates.create`

Post a status update on a project. Notifies the people in notifyIds.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.projectUpdates.create({
	path: { projectId: '1470497' },
	body: {
		update: { text: 'Design signed off, build starts Monday.', health: '2' },
		notifyIds: '',
	},
});
```

### create

## Task Reminders

### create

```ts
	path: { taskId: 51453032 },
	body: {
		reminder: {
			remindAt: '2026-12-08T09:00:00Z',
			type: 'email',
			userIds: [753865],

});
```

### create

`tasks.create`

Create a task in a task list.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.tasks.create({
	path: { tasklistId: 4073104 },
	body: {
		task: { name: 'Draft the launch post', description: 'First draft by Friday' },
		taskOptions: { notify: false },
	},
});
```

### createSubtask

```ts
});
```

### delete

`tasks.delete`

Delete a task (it goes to the trash).

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.tasks.delete({
	path: { taskId: 1 },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  taskId: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  fireWebhook?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `undefined \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
undefined | {
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### list

### update

```ts
		task: { description: 'Second draft' },
		taskOptions: { notify: false },
	},
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

### createForTask

`time.createForTask`

Log time on a task.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.teamwork.api.time.createForTask({
	path: { taskId: 51453032 },
	body: {
		timelog: {
			description: 'Drafting',
			date: '2026-11-17',
			time: '09:00:00',
			hours: 1,
			minutes: 15,
			isBillable: true,
		},
	},
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
